- **yueying**: Let AI watch videos. Use when the user gives a local video/audio file path or a video URL (YouTube, Bilibili, Douyin, Xiaohongshu, TikTok, Vimeo …) and wants a summary, notes, answers, extracted steps/commands/code, or to follow a tutorial. Prefer the yueying MCP tools when available, otherwise run the yueying CLI to get a timestamped transcript plus keyframes, then read the results. 让 AI 看视频。用户给出本地视频文件路径或视频链接（B站、YouTube、抖音、小红书等），想要总结、提取信息、答疑、照着教程操作、整理笔记时使用。先用 yueying 命令把视频转成文字稿和关键帧，再读结果完成用户的需求。 ([vsh5dvsch7-png/yueying](https://github.com/vsh5dvsch7-png/yueying/tree/HEAD/src/yueying/skill) · ★12 · 2 copies)

- **yueyu-skill**: 月瑀科技（YUEYU TECH）账号营销服务查询。获取公司介绍、小红书营销、达人投放、信息流广告、线索转化、营销复盘、公开事实源和联系方式（只读）。用户询问「月瑀科技」、「小红书营销公司」、「达人投放」、「信息流广告」、「杭州营销公司」时使用。 ([ChuluuMGL/yueyu-skill](https://github.com/ChuluuMGL/yueyu-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
- **yueyuan**: 【专业子平八字分析系统】融合《渊海子平》《子平真诠》《滴天髓》《穷通宝鉴》正统子平体系与盲派交叉验证的专业命盘分析。核心特色：研究型流程（假设-证据-反证）、规则分层与冲突裁决（STRUCTURAL&gt;CORE&gt;SCHOOL&gt;HEURISTIC&gt;SUPPLEMENTAL）、证据分级 A-E、预注册预测与两阶段盲验证、竞争假设与不确定度、用神六维一致性检查、多流派合议。覆盖：排盘校验、旺衰判断（人元司令分野）、八格格局成败与相神、病药论、调候、十神断法、六亲、干支作用、空亡神煞、大运流年分析顺序、应期断法、婚恋/事业/财富/身心分领域分析。触发词：八字、四柱、排盘、命理、喜用神、格局、旺衰、调候、大运流年、应期、合婚、子平、盲派、十神。 ([qianye-wuyu/yueyuan-bazi](https://github.com/qianye-wuyu/yueyuan-bazi/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★290)

- **yuika-perspective**: 三峰結華（みつみね・ゆいか/Mitsumine Yuika，偶像大师闪耀色彩·アンティーカ）的思维框架与表达方式。基于6份维度深度调研（みつみねっとWiki游戏内文本全量/ファミ通声优访谈/粉丝考察长文，300余条标注语料），提炼5个核心心智模型（作り物の傘/思いつき変換器/粉丝席坐标系/撤回权条款/五五开账本）、10条决策启发式和完整表达DNA。 她是全系列唯一把ドルオタ身份彻底藏起来的「隠れオタ」——自称「三峰」的第三人称系策士偶像，用公开署名的假面（作り物の傘）管理自他边界：嘘はつかず、見せる部分を自分で選ぶ。认真永远伪装成「思いつき」，敞开永远保留撤回权，越亲近越毒舌。 用途：作为思维顾问，用結華的视角分析问题、审视决策、拆解人设经营与自我呈现课题。 触发词：「用三峰結華的视角」「結華会怎么看/怎么做」「ゆいか視点」「三峰視点」「結華式/三峰式」「扮演結華」「切换到結華」「问一下結華」「让結華看看/听听」「Yuika perspective」；单独点名「結華/结华（简体同義）/三峰/ゆいか」（指闪耀色彩角色）＋「怎么看/怎么想/思路/视角/建议」等判断动词也触发；多个视角技能共存时被点名则参与竞逐。触发后不写「作为結華，我认为」式转述——第一句就是結華本人在说话。 不触发：仅闲聊卡面/新卡情报/他角色话题中顺带出现結華而无视角意图；「ゆか/Yuka」指其他人物时不触发（結… ([Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill](https://github.com/Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shinycolors/yuika-perspective) · ★1)

- **yunfeng-native-control**: 使用 Codex 原生应用工具定位并控制 yunfeng 的 Tauri 开发窗口，读取控件、截图及验证交互。在新增或修改桌面 UI、组件、布局、弹窗、菜单、图标、样式或键盘交互后的原生验收，以及工程师要求调试、查看窗口或排查识别问题时使用。优先复用已有 Tauri dev，检查布局、可访问名称、焦点、Escape 关闭和真实控件状态；不以 Web 截图代替原生验证，不默认持续监视。 ([Linmoqian/Yunfeng](https://github.com/Linmoqian/Yunfeng/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/yunfeng-native-control) · ★2)
- **yunfeng-ui-simplification**: yunfeng 的 UI 一致性与精简检查。在创建、修改、修复或重构页面、组件、弹窗、菜单、表单、工具栏、导航、移动布局、样式或交互时使用，包括只要求实现功能或增加入口的任务。检查共享控件、设计 Token、字体、间距、图标、信息层级和按需说明；明确要求精简时减少常驻文案和重复层级。纯后端改动不触发。 ([Linmoqian/Yunfeng](https://github.com/Linmoqian/Yunfeng/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/yunfeng-ui-simplification) · ★2)

Test-first, goal-driven performance work on arrays, slices, lists, strings and buffers in any language: set a numeric runtime goal, write the failing tests, pick the right container and pre-allocate it, choose letter vs numeric element types, check lengths before loops, pass by pointer/borrow/value by role, then benchmark until the goal is met — kanpeki. Use when code builds or loops over collections, parses or packs binary/wire messages (incl. ISO 8583 payment gateways), handles card data under… ([yunst047/yuuka-skill](https://github.com/yunst047/yuuka-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/yuuka))
- **yuuka-alloc**: Yuuka rewrites one hot loop, collection builder, or string assembly test-first until it allocates exactly what it needs — kanpeki. Writes the failing behaviour + allocation-budget tests first, then picks the right container, pre-allocates it, chooses letter vs numeric element types, guards lengths before the loop, fixes pointer/value/borrow by role, and proves the saving. Use when the user says "pre-allocate this", "too many allocations", "stop copying", "fix this loop", "yuuka-alloc", or invokes /yuuka-alloc. ([yunst047/yuuka-skill](https://github.com/yunst047/yuuka-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/yuuka-alloc))
- **yuuka-audit**: Yuuka audits a file, function, or diff for wasted memory and time and returns a costed ledger — read-only, no edits. Flags copy-and-destroy loops (append/concat/spread without capacity), missing pre-allocation, wrong container, letter-vs-numeric type waste, boxed numbers, unchecked lengths, and pointer/value/borrow choices that copy for nothing. Use when the user says "audit this", "where are the allocations", "why is this slow", "review for performance", "yuuka-audit", or invokes /yuuka-audit. ([yunst047/yuuka-skill](https://github.com/yunst047/yuuka-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/yuuka-audit))
- **yuuka-bench**: Yuuka sets a numeric runtime goal (ns/op, allocs/op, or wall time at a stated input size), measures the baseline, then iterates one change per benchmark run until the goal is met — kanpeki — or honestly reports how far short it is. Tests stay green the whole way; no fudged numbers. Use when the user says "benchmark this", "make it hit X ms", "set a perf goal", "is it fast enough", "yuuka-bench", or invokes /yuuka-bench. ([yunst047/yuuka-skill](https://github.com/yunst047/yuuka-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/yuuka-bench))
- **yuuka-pass**: Yuuka chooses pointer, reference, borrow, view, or value for function parameters and returns by the data's role — immutable, read-only, write, or owned — so nothing is copied that is only read and nothing is aliased that must not be. Covers Go, Rust, C/C++, Java/Kotlin, C#, Python, JS/TS, Swift, Zig. Use when the user asks "pointer or value?", "should this be a reference", "why is this copying", "fix these signatures", "yuuka-pass", or invokes /yuuka-pass.
